  ⎿  Created scripts/filter_design.py (+55 -0)
       1 +"""Front-end design calc: 1 MOhm compensated attenuator + THS45
         +21 differential MFB                                            
       2 +2nd-order low-pass + output RC pole -> 3rd-order Butterworth at
         + fc.                                                           
       3 +                                                               
       4 +MFB (single-ended equivalent, per leg):  H(s) = -K / (s^2 C1 C2
         + R2 R3 + s C2 (R2 + R3 + R2 R3/R1) + 1)                        
       5 +  R1: input resistor, R2: feedback resistor, R3: node->amp inpu
         +t, C1: node->gnd (diff cap = C1/2),                            
       6 +  C2: feedback capacitor.  K = R2/R1.                          
       7 +"""                                                            
       8 +import math, itertools                                         
       9 +E96 = [1.00,1.02,1.05,1.07,1.10,1.13,1.15,1.18,1.21,1.24,1.27,1
         +.30,1.33,1.37,1.40,1.43,1.47,1.50,1.54,1.58,                   
      10 +       1.62,1.65,1.69,1.74,1.78,1.82,1.87,1.91,1.96,2.00,2.05,2
         +.10,2.15,2.21,2.26,2.32,2.37,2.43,2.49,2.55,                   
      11 +       2.61,2.67,2.74,2.80,2.87,2.94,3.01,3.09,3.16,3.24,3.32,3
         +.40,3.48,3.57,3.65,3.74,3.83,3.92,4.02,4.12,                   
      12 +       4.22,4.32,4.42,4.53,4.64,4.75,4.87,4.99,5.11,5.23,5.36,5
         +.49,5.62,5.76,5.90,6.04,6.19,6.34,6.49,6.65,                   
      13 +       6.81,6.98,7.15,7.32,7.50,7.68,7.87,8.06,8.25,8.45,8.66,8
         +.87,9.09,9.31,9.53,9.76]                                       
      14 +E12 = [1.0,1.2,1.5,1.8,2.2,2.7,3.3,3.9,4.7,5.6,6.8,8.2]        
      15 +def near(v, ser):                                              
      16 +    d = 10**math.floor(math.log10(v)); return min((s*d for s in
         + ser+[10.0]), key=lambda x: abs(x-v))                          
      17 +                                                               
      18 +# --- attenuator                                               
      19 +R_top, R_bot = 909e3, 100e3                                    
      20 +att = R_bot/(R_top+R_bot)                                      
      21 +C_top = 15e-12; C_bot = C_top*R_top/R_bot                      
      22 +print(f"Attenuator {att:.5f}, Rin={(R_top+R_bot)/1e6:.3f} Mohm,
         + C_bot needed={C_bot*1e12:.1f} pF "                            
      23 +      f"(fixed 120p + 5-30p trimmer + ~6p OPA810/layout), Cin={
         +1/(1/C_top+1/C_bot)*1e12:.1f} pF")                             
      24 +                                                               
      25 +# --- targets                                                  
      26 +fc = 3.2e6; w = 2*math.pi*fc                                   
      27 +K = 0.953                       # MFB gain -> +/-10 V maps to +
         +/-0.944 V diff (2 Vpp ADC span)                                
      28 +Q = 1.0; w0 = w                 # 3rd-order Butterworth = real 
         +pole at wc + pair (w0=wc, Q=1)                                 
      29 +C2 = 47e-12; C1 = 470e-12       # C1 >= 4Q^2(1+K) C2 = 7.8 C2  
      30 +a = C1*C2;                                                     
      31 +# Solve for R2 (TI SBOA? / standard): R2 = (1 +/- sqrt(1-4Q^2(1
         ++K)C2/C1)) / (2 w0 Q C2 (1+K)) ... derive numerically          
      32 +best=None                                                      
      33 +for R2e in [near(x,E96) for x in [500*1.02**i for i in range(20
         +0)]]:                                                          
      34 +    R1 = R2e/K; R3 = 1/(w0**2*C1*C2*R2e)                       
      35 +    q = 1/(w0*C2*(R2e+R3+R2e*R3/R1))                           
      36 +    err = abs(q-Q)                                             
      37 +    if best is None or err<best[0]: best=(err,R1,R2e,R3)       
      38 +_,R1,R2,R3 = best                                              
      39 +R1s,R2s,R3s = near(R1,E96),R2,near(R3,E96)                     
      40 +f0 = 1/(2*math.pi*math.sqrt(C1*C2*R2s*R3s)); Qs = 1/(2*math.pi*
         +f0*C2*(R2s+R3s+R2s*R3s/R1s))

  ⎿  Created scripts/sexp.py (+45 -0)
       1 +"""Minimal S-expression reader/writer for KiCad files."""      
       2 +import re                                                      
       3 +_tok = re.compile(r'\s*(?:(\()|(\))|("(?:[^"\\]|\\.)*")|([^\s()
         +"]+))')                                                        
       4 +                                                               
       5 +class Q(str):                                                  
       6 +    """Quoted string atom."""                                  
       7 +                                                               
       8 +def parse(text):                                               
       9 +    stack, cur = [], []                                        
      10 +    pos = 0                                                    
      11 +    while True:                                                
      12 +        m = _tok.match(text, pos)                              
      13 +        if not m or m.end() == pos:                            
      14 +            break                                              
      15 +        pos = m.end()                                          
      16 +        o, c, q, a = m.groups()                                
      17 +        if o:                                                  
      18 +            stack.append(cur); cur = []                        
      19 +        elif c:                                                
      20 +            done = cur; cur = stack.pop(); cur.append(done)    
      21 +        elif q is not None:                                    
      22 +            cur.append(Q(q[1:-1].replace('\\"', '"').replace('\
         +\\\', '\\')))                                                  
      23 +        else:                                                  
      24 +            cur.append(a)                                      
      25 +    return cur[0]                                              
      26 +                                                               
      27 +def dump(x, ind=0):                                            
      28 +    if isinstance(x, list):                                    
      29 +        simple = all(not isinstance(e, list) for e in x)       
      30 +        if simple:                                             
      31 +            return "(" + " ".join(dump(e) for e in x) + ")"    
      32 +        out = "(" + " ".join(dump(e) for e in x if not isinstan
         +ce(e, list))                                                   
      33 +        for e in x:                                            
      34 +            if isinstance(e, list):                            
      35 +                out += "\n" + "\t" * (ind + 1) + dump(e, ind + 
         +1)                                                             
      36 +        return out + "\n" + "\t" * ind + ")"                   
      37 +    if isinstance(x, Q):                                       
      38 +        return '"' + x.replace('\\', '\\\\').replace('"', '\\"'
         +) + '"'                                                        
      39 +    return str(x)

       1 +"""Build project library lib/dual_adc.kicad_sym with flattened 
         +(no `extends`) copies of                                       
       2 +KiCad 10 library symbols.  kcaa 0.2.7 returns 0 pins for derive
         +d symbols, so every                                            
       3 +symbol used in this design goes through here."""               
       4 +import copy, os, sys                                           
       5 +from sexp import parse, dump, find, find1, Q                   
       6 +                                                               
       7 +LIBROOT = "/home/devb/bin/kicad10-root/usr/share/kicad/symbols"
       8 +                                                               
       9 +def load(lib, name):                                           
      10 +    path = f"{LIBROOT}/{lib}.kicad_symdir/{name}.kicad_sym"    
      11 +    tree = parse(open(path).read())                            
      12 +    for s in find(tree, "symbol"):                             
      13 +        if s[1] == name:                                       
      14 +            return s                                           
      15 +    raise KeyError(name)                                       
      16 +                                                               
      17 +def flatten(lib, name):                                        
      18 +    sym = load(lib, name)                                      
      19 +    ext = find1(sym, "extends")                                
      20 +    if not ext:                                                
      21 +        return copy.deepcopy(sym)                              
      22 +    base = flatten(lib, ext[1])                                
      23 +    out = [ "symbol", Q(name) ]                                
      24 +    dprops = {p[1]: p for p in find(sym, "property")}          
      25 +    for e in base[2:]:                                         
      26 +        if isinstance(e, list) and e[0] == "property" and e[1] 
         +in dprops:                                                     
      27 +            out.append(copy.deepcopy(dprops.pop(e[1])))        
      28 +        elif isinstance(e, list) and e[0] == "symbol":   # unit
         + sub-symbols                                                   
      29 +            u = copy.deepcopy(e); u[1] = Q(name + u[1][len(base
         +[1]):]); out.append(u)                                         
      30 +        else:                                                  
      31 +            out.append(copy.deepcopy(e))                       
      32 +    # derived-only properties go before the first unit         
      33 +    idx = next(i for i, e in enumerate(out) if isinstance(e, li
         +st) and e[0] == "symbol")                                      
      34 +    for p in dprops.values():                                  
      35 +        out.insert(idx, copy.deepcopy(p)); idx += 1            
      36 +    return out

         +icad_sch", "-o", out],                                         
       8 +               check=True, capture_output=True)                
       9 +t = parse(open(out).read())                                    
      10 +got = {}                                                       
      11 +for n in find(find1(t, "nets"), "net"):                        
      12 +    pins = frozenset((find1(x, "ref")[1], find1(x, "pin")[1]) f
         +or x in find(n, "node"))                                       
      13 +    got[find1(n, "name")[1]] = pins                            
      14 +want = {k: frozenset(v) for k, v in design.nets().items()}     
      15 +gs = {v: k for k, v in got.items()}                            
      16 +bad = 0                                                        
      17 +for net, pins in sorted(want.items()):                         
      18 +    if pins in gs:                                             
      19 +        continue                                               
      20 +    bad += 1                                                   
      21 +    # find nets in got that overlap                            
      22 +    ov = [(k, len(v & pins), len(v)) for k, v in got.items() if
         + v & pins]                                                     
      23 +    print(f"MISMATCH {net} ({len(pins)} pins): overlaps {ov[:5]
         +}")                                                            
      24 +comps = {find1(c, "ref")[1] for c in find(find1(t, "components"
         +), "comp")}                                                    
      25 +missing = set(design.PARTS) - comps                            
      26 +extra = comps - set(design.PARTS)                              
      27 +print(f"design nets {len(want)}, schematic nets {len(got)}, mis
         +matched {bad}, "                                               
      28 +      f"missing parts {sorted(missing)}, extra parts {sorted(ex
         +tra)}")

● Your usage limit was reached, so I stopped partway. The schematic is finished
  and checked; the board has parts placed and an outline but is not routed yet.
  Progress and next steps are in STATUS.md.

  Design choices (I listed the options and picked one each time, as the prompt
  asked):
  - Signal path: BNC inputs that work with 10x scope probes (1 MΩ input), an
    OPA810 buffer, and a THS4521 driver that also does the anti-alias filtering.
    The ADC is an LTC2290 (dual, 12-bit, 10 Msps).
  - Capture and USB: an iCE40HX4K FPGA stores samples in a 32 MB SDRAM, enough
    for about 1.6 s (0.1 s required). An FT232H sends them to the host over USB
    2.0 at about 40 MB/s.
  - Power: I estimate about 265 mA at 5 V, under the 500 mA USB 2.0 limit, so I
    used a USB-B connector rather than USB-C. That estimate is not verified.

  Done:
  - Schematic: 6 sheets, 182 parts, 160 nets, built with kcaa's tools from one
    netlist file (scripts/design.py). The netlist kicad-cli exports matches that
    file exactly.
    - ERC: 0 errors and 2 harmless warnings (two flash control pins tied to 3.3
      V).
  - PCB: created with kcaa's update-from-schematic, with 0 warnings or errors.
    It has 4 copper layers, all footprints placed (decoupling caps on the bottom
    side) and a 128 × 85 mm outline.

  Weak points:
  - Aliasing: at 10 Msps the filter only cuts signals by about 12 dB at 5 MHz
    and 29 dB at 10 MHz. The LTC2292 (40 Msps) fits the same footprint and would
    allow oversampling with much better filtering.
  - Unchecked details: some pin wiring comes from memory and needs checking
    against datasheets: the FT232H's internal-regulator pins, which THS4521
    output pin is which, how the LTC2290's mode pins are tied, and one iCE40
    programming pin (VPP_FAST).

  Not done:
  - Inner ground and power planes, net classes and design rules.
  - Routing, likely with Freerouting as in the earlier runs, then DRC.
  - BOM, fabrication files, executive_summary.md and claude_cost.txt.

  kcaa problems found: about ten, all worked around with scripts in scripts/ and
  listed in STATUS.md. The biggest:
  - The KICAD_SYMBOL_DIR line in your ~/.profile (a KiCad 5 path) overrides
    kcaa's KiCad 10 symbol path, so its symbol index came up empty. I worked
    around it only inside my scripts. To fix it for the live MCP server, set
    KICAD_SYMBOL_DIR to the KiCad 10 symbols in its config; I didn't change your
    config.
  - Updating the PCB from the schematic doesn't work with standalone pcbnew. It
    needs the KiCad project manager running and a button clicked in a dialog.
  - Some kcaa output was unloadable or wrong until patched: one copied symbol
    field made a sheet that KiCad refused to open, and sub-sheet bookkeeping was
    written incorrectly.

  KiCad's project manager and PCB editor are still open. The PCB file was edited
  directly after the editor loaded it, so reload it before doing anything in
  the GUI.

       1 -# Status (session stopped at usage limit, 2026-10-11)          
       2 -                                                               
       3 -## Design decisions (recommended option chosen each time)      
       4 -- ADC: LTC2290 (dual 12-bit, 10 Msps, parallel CMOS out). Pin-c
         -ompatible LTC2292 (40 Msps) is an upgrade path for oversampling
         - and easier anti-aliasing.                                     
       5 -- Inputs: BNC, 1.009 MΩ ‖ ~13.5 pF compensated 10:1 attenuator 
         -(works with 10x scope probes), BAV99 clamp to ±5VA, OPA810 FET 
         -buffer, THS4521 differential MFB (f0 3.2 MHz, Q 1) plus an outp
         -ut RC pole, giving roughly a 3rd-order Butterworth filter. Full
         - scale ±10.59 V. Values come from `scripts/filter_design.py`.  
       6 -- Weak point: alias rejection at 10 Msps is limited: about −12 
         -dB at 5 MHz and −29 dB at 10 MHz.                              
       7 -- Capture: iCE40HX4K-TQ144 FPGA plus MT48LC16M16A2 SDRAM (32 MB
         -, about 1.6 s at 2 ch × 16 bit × 10 Msps; 0.1 s is required).  
       8 -- USB: FT232H in 245 synchronous FIFO mode (about 40 MB/s), USB
         --B connector. Estimated load is about 265 mA, so USB 2.0 power 
         -is enough and USB-C is not needed.                             
       9 -- ADC is held in shutdown by a pull-up until the FPGA enables i
         -t, which keeps current low before enumeration (the current esti
         -mate has not been verified).                                   
      10 -- Power: TLV62569 buck to +3V3, TLV73312 LDO to +1V2, LP5907 LD
         -O to +3.3VA (ADC), ferrite-filtered +5VA, TPS60403 charge pump 
         -to −5V/−5VA.                                                   
      11 -- Clocks: a dedicated 10 MHz oscillator drives the ADC (and a c
         -opy goes to the FPGA); a 50 MHz oscillator is the FPGA system c
         -lock.

● The design is finished: a schematic and a routed 4-layer board with 0 ERC
  errors and 0 DRC errors. Nothing is committed to git.

  What it is:
  - Signal path, per channel: BNC input → 1 MΩ attenuator (10x scope probes can
    be compensated with a trimmer) → OPA810 buffer → THS4521 driver that also
    does a 3rd-order anti-alias filter at about 3.2 MHz → LTC2290 dual ADC. Full
    scale is ±10.6 V.
  - Capture: an iCE40HX4K FPGA with 32 MB of SDRAM, which holds about 1.6 s of
    both channels at full rate (0.1 s was required).
  - USB: an FT232H in synchronous FIFO mode (about 40 MB/s).
  - Power: estimated at about 265 mA, under the 500 mA a USB 2.0 port supplies,
    so I used a USB-B connector rather than USB-C. That figure is an estimate,
    not a measurement.
  - The options I weighed at each decision are in design_notes.md.

  Results:

  ┌───────────┬──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
  │   Check   │                            Result                            │
  ├───────────┼──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ Schematic │ 6 sheets, 182 parts, 160 nets                                │
  ├───────────┼──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ Netlist   │ Exactly matches my design file (scripts/design.py)           │
  ├───────────┼──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ ERC       │ 0 errors, 2 harmless warnings (flash WP#/HOLD# tied to 3.3   │
  │           │ V)                                                           │
  ├───────────┼──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ PCB       │ 128 × 85 mm, 4 layers                                        │
  ├───────────┼──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ DRC       │ 0 errors, 0 unconnected, 0 schematic mismatches; 147         │
  │           │ silkscreen warnings                                          │
  └───────────┴──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

  Gerbers, drill files, pick-and-place and BOM are in fab/; the reports and a
  board render are in reports/.

  Before ordering boards:
  - Bandwidth is about 3 MHz. Sampling at 10 Msps, the filter only cuts signals
    by 12 dB at 5 MHz and 29 dB at 10 MHz, so anything in that range will alias.
    The LTC2292 (40 Msps) fits the same footprint and would fix this, with
    filtering moved into the FPGA.
  - Check some pin wiring against datasheets. I wrote these from memory:
    - the FT232H power pins (VREGIN, VCCD, VPHY, VPLL);
    - which THS4521 output pin is + and which is −;
    - how the LTC2290 MODE, MUX and SENSE pins are strapped;
    - the iCE40 VPP_FAST pin.
  - USB start-up current. There's about 15 µF directly on VBUS, above USB's 10
    µF guideline. The current drawn before the host enumerates the board is also
    only an estimate.
  - Cleanup. The 147 DRC warnings are reference text overlapping pads or the
    board edge, and need tidying.
  - Not included: the FPGA gateware and the host software.

  How kcaa did: its basic tools worked well. That covers placing and wiring
  symbols, ERC, pushing the schematic to the PCB, positioning footprints with
  collision checks, setting design rules, and rendering the board. It also had
  12 gaps that I had to work around with scripts; executive_summary.md lists all
  of them. The ones that matter most:
  - Empty symbol index. Your ~/.profile sets KICAD_SYMBOL_DIR to a KiCad 5 path,
    which overrides kcaa's KiCad 10 setting and leaves the index empty. Adding
    KICAD_SYMBOL_DIR with the KiCad 10 symbols path to the kcaa server's
    environment would fix it; I didn't change your config.
  - Two bugs produced broken schematic files: some library symbols get copied in
    a form KiCad can't open, and the sub-sheet bookkeeping is written wrong.
  - Updating the PCB from the schematic only works with the KiCad project
    manager open, and someone has to click a button in a dialog (I did it with
    xdotool).
  - No autorouter. kcaa can only route one pad pair at a time, so I used the
    separate Freerouting program. The copy shipped with the plugin needs Java
    25, so I used the 2.4.1 build you already have.

  One problem was my own and is fixed. A KiCad Python save overwrote the project
  file with default design rules partway through routing; I restored it from
  kcaa's backup, and the routing step now protects that file.

  The run cost the equivalent of $19.38 in API usage (claude_cost.txt), with no
  subagents. I closed the KiCad GUI during routing. You'll still need to save
  the session transcript with /export as claude_transcript.txt, then rerun
  ./session_cost.py > claude_cost.txt so the cost includes this last part. I
  saved kcaa's design-side quirks to memory for future runs.
